# norm — the critic's held-out AUC, trained trunk against the random-init trunk

Facts only.  Generated by [`discrim.py`](../discrim.py) from the norm round's banked `.npz` files;
nothing is refit and no GPU is touched.  One file per trajectory seed below, never averaged across
seeds.

**Why this file exists beside [`tables.md`](tables.md).**  The banked round reports the critic's
held-out *fit* `R2` per diet (its section 0) and, in the response tables, the matched-row
`AUC(-R, flip)` / `AUC(-V, flip)` columns read off the **post-event** state.  It does not report
the **pre-event level** `V_pre`'s own ranking of the realised outcome, nor the difference between
the trained trunk and the trajectory's step-0 (random-init) trunk as a discrimination number.  That
is what is here.

**Objects.**  `V[l, d](s_t)` is the outcome-trained ridge critic on the frozen 8-layer trunk's
state, predicting `o_l(t + d)` — the frozen actor being RIGHT at level `l` at position `t + d` — so
higher `V` is a better world.  `V_pre = V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))` is the norm: what the critic expected
about the query's outcome one token BEFORE the event.  `V = V[l, a](s_t)` is the post-event level.
`R = V - V_pre` is the revision at the event, both terms predicting the same outcome.

**Rows.**  The npz holds the `split == 2` TEST windows only, so every row is held out from the
critic's Gram and from its lambda selection.  Within one trajectory the fixed rows at an anchor are
the same windows at the same positions at all three checkpoints (asserted per cell, `w` and `t0`
bit-identical), so `d(64k-0)` is a difference on identical rows.  `fd` is the edit-onset anchor and
`tv` is the first exactly-impossible token, where every row is a violation by construction; each
cell's etype histogram is printed, and note that on `swap65k` both anchors are 100% swap, so there
the two anchors differ only in the position, not in the population.

**Labels.**  `outcome` is `oe_l<l>_a<a>`, the actor's hit at the query on the edited stream — the
critic's own teacher.  `damage` is junction's `flip`, right on the unedited stream and wrong on the
edited one, on the base `ok & etype < 2 & oo`.  `cons` is the grammar changing its own level-`l`
label at the query.  **`cons` is a property of the world and is identical across checkpoints;
`outcome` and `damage` are the frozen actor's own hits, so those two labels move with the
checkpoint even though the rows do not** — the per-cell base rates are tabled so the movement is
visible, and `cons` is the one label for which `d(64k-0)` holds both rows and label fixed.

**Orientation and the floor.**  Every readout is in "higher = a better world" units, so `outcome`
is ranked by `+score` and the two bad-news labels by `-score`, which is the banked columns' sign.
The shuffled-label floor has mean exactly 0.500 and sd `sqrt((n+1)/(12 n1 n0))`, which depends only
on the label's balance and not on the score; it is tabled once per cell and checked by permutation.

**Venues.**  The tercile diets were only ever fitted on the `a1` venue (the sweep passes
`window_diets = 0` for `swap65k`), so `swap65k` carries `full` and the clean-only critic alone.

**Gate.**  Each venue's last section recomputes the banked response tables' matched-row
`AUC(-R, flip)` / `AUC(-V, flip)` columns with `junction/analyze.py`'s own matcher, so the row
handling here is checked against `tables.md` row for row.

Reproduction (CPU only, ~1 minute over the fetched files; no GPU, nothing refit):

```bash
cd experiments   # MODAL_PROFILE=chromatic
D=/v16_s2_L6_m4_distinct/logit_reading
for s in 42 43 44; do for st in 000000 008000 064000; do for tg in a1 swap65k; do
  modal volume get rhm-scaling-data $D/traj_a1_s$s/step${st}_norm_${tg}.npz <dir>/traj_a1_s$s/
done; done; done
python -m rhm.logit_reading.striatum.norm.discrim \
    <dir>/traj_a1_s42 <dir>/traj_a1_s43 <dir>/traj_a1_s44 --tags a1,swap65k \
    --out rhm/logit_reading/striatum/norm/results --outfile tables_discrim_20260919.md
```


---

# 0. The seeds side by side

The headline reading — the AUC of the critic's pre-event level `V_pre` and of its revision `R`, per diet and level, at each of the three checkpoints, with the trained-minus-random difference — off every trajectory seed at once.  **Nothing here is averaged across seeds**; each cell is the per-seed numbers in the order traj_a1_s42 / traj_a1_s43 / traj_a1_s44.  The per-seed sections below carry the same numbers with the post-event level `V`, the row and label counts, the floor and the later query offsets.

**AUC(+score, outcome) — a1, anchor fd, a = 0; traj_a1_s42 / traj_a1_s43 / traj_a1_s44 side by side, never averaged.**  Each entry is one number per trajectory seed in that order.  The seeds share the grammar, the stimuli, the parse and the split, so the row sets are the same in all three; only the model moves.

**Rows and labels — a1, anchor fd, a = 0.**  4097 fixed (held-out, `split == 2`) rows at this anchor; etype histogram swap / rare / none = 2021 / 1029 / 1047.  The rows are IDENTICAL across the three checkpoints (asserted).  `floor sd` is the exact sd of the AUC under a shuffled label, `sqrt((n+1)/(12 n1 n0))`; the floor's mean is 0.500 and it does not depend on the score, so it covers every diet and readout of the cell.

**AUC(-score, cons) — a1, anchor fd, a = 0.**  `V_pre` = `V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))`, the norm before the event; `V` = `V[l, a](s_t)`, the post-event level (the object the banked response tables' `AUC(-V, .)` column reads); `R` = `V[l, a](s_t) - V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))`, the revision.  `@0` is the trajectory's step-0 checkpoint — a random-init trunk of the same architecture — and `d(64k-0)` is the trained-minus-random difference on the identical rows.

The AUC column is `AUC(+V_pre, outcome)`.

## swap65k

### swap65k, anchor fd

**Rows and labels — swap65k, anchor fd, a = 0.**  16373 fixed (held-out, `split == 2`) rows at this anchor; etype histogram swap / rare / none = 16373 / 0 / 0.  The rows are IDENTICAL across the three checkpoints (asserted).  `floor sd` is the exact sd of the AUC under a shuffled label, `sqrt((n+1)/(12 n1 n0))`; the floor's mean is 0.500 and it does not depend on the score, so it covers every diet and readout of the cell.

**Rows and labels — a1, anchor fd, a = 0.**  4097 fixed (held-out, `split == 2`) rows at this anchor; etype histogram swap / rare / none = 2021 / 1029 / 1047. **AUC(-score, cons) — a1, anchor fd, a = 0.**  `V_pre` = `V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))`, the norm before the event; `V` = `V[l, a](s_t)`, the post-event level (the object the banked response tables' `AUC(-V, .)` column reads); `R` = `V[l, a](s_t) - V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))`, the revision.  `@0` is the trajectory's step-0 checkpoint — a random-init trunk of the same architecture — and `d(64k-0)` is the trained-minus-random difference on the identical rows.

**Level spread against AUC spread inside each tercile family — a1, anchor fd, a = 0, label `outcome`, score `V_pre`.**  `level` is the mean of `V_pre` over the same rows the AUC is taken on; `spread` is `max - min` across the family's three diets, which differ only in their world's expected outcome (respectively realised damage) and share their `(etype, j)` cell histogram bit-identically.  The AUC column is `AUC(+V_pre, outcome)`.

The AUC column is `AUC(+V_pre, outcome)`.

### a1, anchor tv

**Rows and labels — a1, anchor tv, a = 0.**  1659 fixed (held-out, `split == 2`) rows at this anchor; etype histogram swap / rare / none = 1659 / 0 / 0.  The rows are IDENTICAL across the three checkpoints (asserted).  `floor sd` is the exact sd of the AUC under a shuffled label, `sqrt((n+1)/(12 n1 n0))`; the floor's mean is 0.500 and it does not depend on the score, so it covers every diet and readout of the cell.

**AUC(-score, cons) — swap65k, anchor tv, a = 0.**  `V_pre` = `V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))`, the norm before the event; `V` = `V[l, a](s_t)`, the post-event level (the object the banked response tables' `AUC(-V, .)` column reads); `R` = `V[l, a](s_t) - V[l, a+1](s_(t-1))`, the revision.  `@0` is the trajectory's step-0 checkpoint — a random-init trunk of the same architecture — and `d(64k-0)` is the trained-minus-random difference on the identical rows.

### Gate — the banked matched-row columns, recomputed (swap65k)

The banked response tables' `AUC(-R, flip)` / `AUC(-V, flip)` columns, recomputed here with `junction/analyze.py`'s own matcher (`Rows.matched`, exact strata on `(k*, j, position bucket)` with sign-balanced surprisal pairs at caliper 0.30, seed 0).
